B.10 code/q3_balance.py
1 """Continuous‐solution balance integrals for the accepted Q3 effective model."""
2 import numpy as np
3 from numpy.polynomial.legendre import leggauss
4 from appendix3 import heat_capacity, heat_capacity_deriv, moisture_diffusivity_q2
5 from utils import R, L, H, HM
6
7
8 def audit_segment(fvm, sol, Ta_fun, Ca_fun, t_end=None, orders=(4,8)):
9 if sol.sol is None:
10 raise ValueError("balance audit requires a dense solution")
11 n = fvm.n_nodes
12 start = float(sol.sol.ts[0])
13 end = float(sol.sol.ts[‐1]) if t_end is None else float(t_end)
14 if not start < end <= sol.sol.ts[‐1]+1e‐9:
15 raise ValueError("audit outside dense‐solution interval")
16 edges = np.r_[sol.sol.ts[sol.sol.ts < end],end]
17 edges = np.unique(edges)
18 z0, z1 = sol.sol(start), sol.sol(end)
19 factor = 2*np.pi*L
20 def energy(z):
21 return float(factor*np.dot(fvm.W,heat_capacity(z[n:])*(z[:n]‐28.)))
22 def quadrature(order):
23 gx,gw=leggauss(order)
24 total=np.zeros(3)
25 for j in range(0,len(edges)‐1,64):
26 aa,bb=edges[j:‐1][:64],edges[j+1:][:64]
27 tt=((aa+bb)[:,None]/2+(bb‐aa)[:,None]/2*gx).ravel()
28 Y=sol.sol(tt);T,C=Y[:n],Y[n:]
29 Ta,Ca=np.asarray(Ta_fun(tt)),np.asarray(Ca_fun(tt))
30 D=moisture_diffusivity_q2(C,T)
31 Df=2*D[:‐1]*D[1:]/np.maximum(D[:‐1]+D[1:],1e‐300)
32 F=‐fvm.r_half[:,None]*Df*np.diff(C,axis=0)/fvm.dr_face[:,None]
33 flows=np.vstack([np.zeros((1,len(tt))),F,R*HM*(C[‐1]‐Ca)])
34 Cdot=‐np.diff(flows,axis=0)/fvm.W[:,None]
35 vals=np.vstack([R*HM*(C[‐1]‐Ca),factor*R*H*(Ta‐T[‐1]),
36 factor*np.sum(fvm.W[:,None]*heat_capacity_deriv(C)*(T‐28.)*Cdot,axis=0)])
37 weights=((bb‐aa)[:,None]/2*gw).ravel()
38 total+=vals@weights
39 return total
40 integrals={str(o):quadrature(o).tolist() for o in orders}
41 return {"start_s":start,"end_s":end,"accepted_intervals":len(edges)‐1,
42 "M_start":float(fvm.W@z0[n:]),"M_end":float(fvm.W@z1[n:]),
43 "E_start":energy(z0),"E_end":energy(z1),"integrals_by_order":integrals}
44
45
46 def combine(parts):
47 if not parts:
48 raise ValueError('no balance segments')
49 for left, right in zip(parts, parts[1:]):
50 if abs(left['end_s']‐right['start_s']) > 1e‐9:
51 raise ValueError('balance segments have a time gap or overlap')
52 for field in ('M', 'E'):
53 if not np.isclose(left[field+'_end'], right[field+'_start'], rtol=1e‐12, atol=1e‐14):
54 raise ValueError('balance segments have a state discontinuity')
55 I=np.sum([p['integrals_by_order']['8'] for p in parts],axis=0)
56 I4=np.sum([p['integrals_by_order']['4'] for p in parts],axis=0)
57 M0,M1=parts[0]['M_start'],parts[‐1]['M_end']
58 E0,E1=parts[0]['E_start'],parts[‐1]['E_end']
59 mass_denom=max(abs(M0‐M1),abs(I[0]),1e‐18)
60 heat_denom=max(abs(E1‐E0‐I[2]),abs(I[1]),1e‐12)
61 return {
62 'moisture':{'M0':M0,'M_end':M1,'surface_integral':float(I[0]),
63 'relative_residual':float(abs(M1‐M0+I[0])/mass_denom)},
74
64 'heat':{'E_end_minus_E0':E1‐E0,'composition_correction':float(I[2]),'Q_surface':float(I[1]),
65 'relative_residual':float(abs(E1‐E0‐I[2]‐I[1])/heat_denom)},
66 'quadrature_relative_difference':{'moisture':float(abs(I[0]‐I4[0])/mass_denom),
67 'heat_and_composition':float((abs(I[1]‐I4[1])+abs(I[2]‐I4[2]))/heat_denom)},
68 'parts':parts,
69 'interpretation':'Integrated effective equation with B_prime(C)*(T‐28)*Cdot correction; not full
,→ multiphase energy conservation.'}

50 def compare_fields(a,b):
51 """Shared physical nodes and exactly matching stored times."""
75
52 times,ia,ib=np.intersect1d(a['times'],b['times'],return_indices=True)
53 ra,rb=a['fvm'].r,b['fvm'].r
54 j=np.searchsorted(rb,ra)
55 if np.max(abs(rb[j]‐ra))>1e‐13:
56 raise RuntimeError('spatial meshes are not nested')
57 na,nb=a['n_nodes'],b['n_nodes']
58 # Batch to bound temporary array sizes for fine meshes.
59 maxT=maxC=0.
60 for start in range(0,len(times),128):
61 ai,bi=ia[start:start+128],ib[start:start+128]
62 maxT=max(maxT,float(np.max(abs(a['Y'][ai,:na]‐b['Y'][bi,:nb][:,j]))))
63 maxC=max(maxC,float(np.max(abs(a['Y'][ai,na:]‐b['Y'][bi,nb:][:,j]))))
64 return {'matched_times':len(times),'max_T':maxT,'max_C':maxC}

67 def main(argv=None):
68 from problem3 import (load_q3_inputs,run_case,run_probe,RTOL,ATOL_T,ATOL_C,MAX_STEP_EARLY,
69 MAX_STEP_LATE,find_strict_report,build_output_tables,physical_bounds,overlap_q2,
70 write_result3_xlsx,write_table5_csv,plot_figures,setup_mpl,conservation_heat,conservation_moisture)
71 parser=argparse.ArgumentParser();parser.add_argument('‐‐probe‐only',action='store_true')
72 parser.add_argument('‐‐no‐xlsx',action='store_true',help='Save gated numerical evidence; export
,→ separately later.')
73 args=parser.parse_args(argv)
74 DIAG.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
75 start=time.perf_counter()
76 Ta,Ca,knots=load_q3_inputs()
77 if args.probe_only:
78 print(json.dumps(run_probe(Ta,Ca)));return
79 cases={}
80 for level in (4,8,16):
81 cases[level]=run_case(level,Ta,Ca,72*3600.,RTOL,ATOL_T,ATOL_C,MAX_STEP_EARLY,MAX_STEP_LATE,
82 tag=f'G{level}',audit_balances=(level==8))
83 save_case(cases[level])
84 pub,ref=cases[8],cases[16]
85 tight=run_case(8,Ta,Ca,72*3600.,RTOL/5,ATOL_T/5,ATOL_C/5,MAX_STEP_EARLY/2,MAX_STEP_LATE/2,tag='G8t')
86 save_case(tight)
87 space=compare_fields(pub,ref);temporal=compare_fields(pub,tight)
88 coarse_space=compare_fields(cases[4],pub)
89 critical=pub['t_star']
90 same={name:float(case['eval_M'](critical)) for name,case in
,→ [('G4',cases[4]),('G8',pub),('G16',ref),('G8t',tight)]}
91 # The maximum differences are measured at the same physical time.
92 err=max(abs(same['G8']‐same['G16']),abs(same['G8']‐same['G8t']))
93 margin=2*err+1e‐8
94 zero_report=find_strict_report(pub['eval_M'],critical,critical+3500,margin=0.)

B.12 code/problem4.py

32 def properties(C, T):
33 C, T = np.asarray(C), np.asarray(T)
34 # Only protect nonphysical implicit‐iteration probes; stored states are audited.
35 Cp = np.maximum(C, 1e‐12)
36 rho = 760.0 + 90.0 * Cp
37 cp = 1850.0 + 2150.0 * Cp / (1.0 + Cp)
38 k = 0.12 + 0.20 * Cp / (1.0 + Cp)
39 D = 4.2e‐4 * np.exp(‐0.30 / Cp) * np.exp(‐3850.0 / (T + 273.15))
40 return rho * cp, k, D

43 class Inputs:
44 def __init__(self):
45 t, Ta, Ca = load_oven_table()
46 if t.size != 241 or t[0] != 0 or t[‐1] != SWITCH:
47 raise ValueError("unexpected oven input coverage")
48 self.Ta = PchipInterpolator(t, Ta, extrapolate=False)
49 self.Ca = PchipInterpolator(t, Ca, extrapolate=False)
50 wb = load_workbook(ATTACH_DIR / " 附件 2.xlsx", read_only=True, data_only=True)
51 radius_rows = list(wb.active.values)
52 wb.close()
53 a = np.asarray(radius_rows[1:], dtype=float)
54 self.rt, self.rv = a[:, 0], a[:, 1] / 100.0
78
55 if (a.shape != (145, 2) or not np.all(np.isfinite(a))
56 or np.any(np.diff(self.rt) <= 0) or np.any(np.diff(self.rv) > 0)
57 or self.rt[0] != 0 or self.rt[‐1] != CAP or self.rv[0] != R):
58 raise ValueError("unexpected radius input")
59 self.radius = PchipInterpolator(self.rt, self.rv, extrapolate=False)
60 self.audit = {"radius_records": len(a), "radius_range_m": [float(self.rv[0]), float(self.rv[‐1])],
61 "radius_cap_s": CAP, "oven_records": len(t), "radius_input_headers":
,→ list(radius_rows[0]),
62 "radius_node_error_m": float(np.max(abs(self.radius(self.rt) ‐ self.rv))),
63 "oven_node_error": float(max(np.max(abs(self.Ta(t)‐Ta)),
,→ np.max(abs(self.Ca(t)‐Ca))))}
64 self.audit["source_sha256"] = {
65 name: hashlib.sha256((ATTACH_DIR / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
66 for name in (" 附件 1.xlsx", " 附件 2.xlsx")}

68 def environment(self, t, late=False):
69 if late or t > SWITCH:
70 return 50.0, 0.05
71 return float(self.Ta(t)), float(self.Ca(t))

74 class ShrinkingFVM:
75 def __init__(self, xi):
76 self.xi = np.asarray(xi, dtype=float)
77 if self.xi[0] != 0 or self.xi[‐1] != 1 or np.any(np.diff(self.xi) <= 0):
78 raise ValueError("invalid material mesh")
79 self.n = len(xi)
80 self.faces = (self.xi[:‐1] + self.xi[1:]) / 2
81 edges = np.r_[0., self.faces, 1.]
82 self.w = np.diff(edges**2) / 2
83 self.dx = np.diff(self.xi)
84
85 def sparsity(self):
86 J = lil_matrix((2*self.n, 2*self.n))
87 for i in range(self.n):
88 for j in range(max(0, i‐1), min(self.n, i+2)):
89 J[i, j] = J[i, self.n+j] = J[self.n+i, j] = J[self.n+i, self.n+j] = 1
90 return J.tocsc()
91
92 def rhs(self, y, radius, Ta, Ca, hm=HM, ht=H):
93 T, C = y[:self.n], y[self.n:]
94 B, k, D = properties(C, T)
95 FT = np.r_[0., ‐self.faces*harmonic_mean(k[:‐1], k[1:])*np.diff(T)/self.dx,
96 radius*ht*(T[‐1]‐Ta)]
97 FC = np.r_[0., ‐self.faces*harmonic_mean(D[:‐1], D[1:])*np.diff(C)/self.dx,
98 radius*hm*(C[‐1]‐Ca)]
99 Tdot = ‐np.diff(FT) / (radius**2 * self.w * B)
100 Cdot = ‐np.diff(FC) / (radius**2 * self.w)
101 return np.r_[Tdot, Cdot]
102

104 def model_checks(inputs):
105 fvm = ShrinkingFVM(np.linspace(0., 1., 41))
106 x = fvm.xi
107 y = np.r_[35.+10*x*x, 1.7‐1.2*x*x]
108 rr = float(inputs.radius(21600.))
109 dy = fvm.rhs(y, rr, 50., 0.05)
110 mass_error = abs(2*np.dot(fvm.w, dy[fvm.n:]) + 2*HM/rr*(y[‐1]‐0.05))
111 B, k, D = properties(y[fvm.n:], y[:fvm.n])
112 heat_error = abs(np.dot(fvm.w*B, dy[:fvm.n]) + H/rr*(y[fvm.n‐1]‐50.))
113 # Independent dimensional‐volume assembly on the same physical annuli.
114 rp = rr*x
115 rf = (rp[:‐1]+rp[1:])/2
116 volumes = np.diff(np.r_[0., rf, rr]**2)/2
79
117 flows_T = np.r_[0., ‐rf*harmonic_mean(k[:‐1], k[1:])*np.diff(y[:fvm.n])/np.diff(rp),
,→ rr*H*(y[fvm.n‐1]‐50.)]
118 flows_C = np.r_[0., ‐rf*harmonic_mean(D[:‐1], D[1:])*np.diff(y[fvm.n:])/np.diff(rp),
,→ rr*HM*(y[‐1]‐0.05)]
119 reference = np.r_[‐np.diff(flows_T)/(volumes*B), ‐np.diff(flows_C)/volumes]
120 fixed_error = float(np.max(abs(dy‐reference)))
121 uniform = np.r_[np.full(fvm.n, T0), np.full(fvm.n, C0)]
122 pure = solve_ivp(lambda t,z: fvm.rhs(z, float(inputs.radius(t)), T0, C0, hm=0., ht=0.),
123 (0., CAP), uniform, method="BDF", jac_sparsity=fvm.sparsity(),
124 rtol=1e‐9, atol=1e‐11)
125 pure_error = float(np.max(abs(pure.y‐uniform[:,None])))
126 checks = {"instant_mass_abs": float(mass_error), "instant_heat_abs": float(heat_error),
127 "fixed_physical_operator_abs": fixed_error, "pure_shrinkage_uniform_abs": pure_error,
128 "pure_shrinkage_success": bool(pure.success)}
129 checks["pass"] = bool(mass_error < 1e‐12 and heat_error < 1e‐6 and fixed_error < 1e‐10
130 and pure_error < 1e‐10 and pure.success)
131 return checks

134 def run(level=2, tight=False):
135 OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
136 inputs = Inputs()
137 basic = model_checks(inputs)
138 if not basic["pass"]:
139 raise RuntimeError(f"model checks failed: {basic}")
140 fvm = ShrinkingFVM(graded_radial_nodes(level)/R)
141 n = fvm.n
142 rtol = 1e‐9 if tight else 1e‐8
143 atol = np.r_[np.full(n, 1e‐9 if tight else 1e‐8), np.full(n, 1e‐11 if tight else 1e‐10)]
144 label = f"G{level}" + ("_tight" if tight else "")
145 y0 = np.r_[np.full(n, T0), np.full(n, C0)]
146 times, states = [np.array([0.])], [y0[None,:]]
147 segments, events, event_states = [], [], []
148 mass_loss = 0.
149 max_mass_error = 0.
150 bounds = [float("inf"), ‐float("inf"), float("inf"), ‐float("inf")]
151 radial_increase = 0.
152 heat_residual_max = 0.
153 wall = time.perf_counter()
154 event = lambda t,y: float(np.max(y[n:])‐THRESHOLD)
155 event.direction = ‐1.
156 event.terminal = False
157 breaks = np.unique(np.r_[0., SWITCH, np.arange(21600., CAP+1., 21600.)])
158 gx, gw = leggauss(4)
159 for a,b in zip(breaks[:‐1], breaks[1:]):
160 late = a >= SWITCH
161 def rhs(t,y):
162 Ta,Ca = inputs.environment(t, late=late)
163 return fvm.rhs(y, float(inputs.radius(t)), Ta, Ca)
164 max_step = (5. if tight else 10.) if not late else (30. if tight else 60.)
165 sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (a,b), y0, method="BDF", rtol=rtol, atol=atol,
166 jac_sparsity=fvm.sparsity(), max_step=max_step,
167 dense_output=True, events=event)
168 if not sol.success:
169 raise RuntimeError(sol.message)
170 ts = np.arange(a+60., b+0.1, 60.)
171 ys = sol.sol(ts).T
172 times.append(ts)
173 states.append(ys)
174 if len(sol.t_events[0]):
175 events.extend(sol.t_events[0].tolist())
176 event_states.extend(sol.y_events[0].tolist())
177 # Quadrature on accepted BDF step intervals; 4‐point Gaussian rule.
178 integral = 0.
179 for start in range(0, len(sol.t)‐1, 128):
80
180 aa, bb = sol.t[start:‐1][:128], sol.t[start+1:][:128]
181 qq = ((aa+bb)[:,None]/2 + (bb‐aa)[:,None]/2*gx).ravel()
182 surface = sol.sol(qq)[‐1]
183 ca = np.full_like(qq, 0.05) if late else inputs.Ca(qq)
184 loss = 2*HM/inputs.radius(qq)*(surface‐ca)
185 integral += float(np.sum(loss.reshape(‐1,4)*gw*(bb‐aa)[:,None]/2))
186 mass_loss += integral
187 y0 = sol.y[:,‐1]
188 mass_error = abs(float(2*np.dot(fvm.w,y0[n:])‐C0+mass_loss))
189 max_mass_error = max(max_mass_error, mass_error)
190 TT, CC = sol.y[:n], sol.y[n:]
191 bounds = [min(bounds[0],float(TT.min())), max(bounds[1],float(TT.max())),
192 min(bounds[2],float(CC.min())), max(bounds[3],float(CC.max()))]
193 radial_increase = max(radial_increase,float(np.max(np.diff(CC,axis=0))))
194 # Audit sampled heat‐equation weighted residual, not d(rho*cp*T)/dt.
195 for tcheck in (a,(a+b)/2,b):
196 z = sol.sol(tcheck)
197 dz = rhs(tcheck,z)
198 B,_,_ = properties(z[n:],z[:n])
199 Ta,_ = inputs.environment(tcheck,late=late)
200 heat_error = abs(float(np.dot(fvm.w*B,dz[:n])+H/float(inputs.radius(tcheck))*(z[n‐1]‐Ta)))
201 heat_residual_max = max(heat_residual_max,heat_error)
202 segments.append({"start_s":float(a),"end_s":float(b),"steps":len(sol.t)‐1,
203 "nfev":sol.nfev,"nlu":sol.nlu,"mass_abs_error":mass_error})
204 print(f"{label}: {b/3600:g}h maxC={y0[n:].max():.8f} mass_err={mass_error:.2e}
,→ wall={time.perf_counter()‐wall:.1f}s",flush=True)
205 tt, yy = np.concatenate(times), np.vstack(states)
206 meta = {"label":label,"level":level,"tight":tight,"nodes":n,"rtol":rtol,
207 "atol_T":float(atol[0]),"atol_C":float(atol[n]),"early_max_step_s":5. if tight else 10.,
208 "late_max_step_s":30. if tight else 60.,"event_s":events[0] if events else None,
209 "event_h":events[0]/3600 if events else None,"end_maxC":float(yy[‐1,n:].max()),
210 "max_mass_abs_error":max_mass_error,"mass_relative_error":max_mass_error/C0,
211 "heat_equation_residual_max":heat_residual_max,"bounds_Tmin_Tmax_Cmin_Cmax":bounds,
212 "max_outward_C_increase":radial_increase,"segments":segments,"input_audit":inputs.audit,
213 "model_checks":basic,"wall_s":time.perf_counter()‐wall,
214 "python":platform.python_version(),"numpy":np.__version__,"scipy":scipy.__version__,
215 "model":"q4‐model‐v1","radius_extrapolation":False,
216 "source_code_sha256":hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
217 np.savez_compressed(OUT/f"{label}.npz",times_s=tt,Y=yy,xi=fvm.xi,w=fvm.w,
218 radius_m=inputs.radius(tt),events_s=np.array(events),event_states=np.array(event_s ⌋
,→ tates))
219 (OUT/f"{label}.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding="utf‐8")
220 print(json.dumps({k:meta[k] for k in
,→ ("label","event_h","end_maxC","mass_relative_error","wall_s")}),flush=True)
221 return meta

B.13 code/verify_q4_results.py

16 return np.load(OUT/f"{label}.npz"), json.loads((OUT/f"{label}.json").read_text(encoding="utf‐8"))

19 def state_at(data, meta, t, inputs):
20 """Match a new time via short BDF restart from a saved full state, not nearest‐time comparison."""
21 times = data["times_s"]
22 i = int(np.searchsorted(times,t,side="right")‐1)
23 if abs(times[i]‐t) < 1e‐8:
24 return data["Y"][i].copy()
25 f = ShrinkingFVM(data["xi"])
26 n = f.n
27 assert times[i] >= SWITCH # New event/report times are in the late constant environment.
28 sol = solve_ivp(lambda tt,y:f.rhs(y,float(inputs.radius(tt)),50.,0.05),
29 (times[i],t),data["Y"][i],method="BDF",jac_sparsity=f.sparsity(),
30 rtol=min(meta["rtol"],1e‐10),atol=np.r_[np.full(n,1e‐10),np.full(n,1e‐12)],
31 max_step=1.)
32 if not sol.success:
33 raise RuntimeError(sol.message)
34 return sol.y[:,‐1]

37 def physical_values(xi, Y, times, inputs, spacing=.001):
38 n = len(xi)
39 fixed = np.arange(21)*.001 if spacing == .001 else np.arange(5)*.005
40 radii = inputs.radius(times)
41 out = np.full((len(times),len(fixed)+1),np.nan)

,→ [2]

128 def modal():
129 # Closed integration of the first PCHIP derivative polynomial at t=1 s.
130 # This avoids the time integrator used by the production modal reference.
131 import problem2 as p
132 from scipy.special import hyp1f1
133 _,env,_=p.load_q2_inputs()
134 coeff=env._pchip.c[:,0]
135 D0=float(p.moisture_diffusivity_q2(p.C0,p.T0))
90
136 values={}
137 for n in [80,160,320,640]:
138 ref=p.BesselDuhamel(p.HM*p.R/D0,D0,p.R,n_terms=n)
139 beta=ref.beta
140 moments=[hyp1f1(1,k+2,‐beta)/(k+1) for k in range(3)]
141 modes=(float(env(0))‐p.C0)*np.exp(‐beta)+coeff[2]*moments[0]+2*coeff[1]*moments[1]+3*coeff[0]*mome ⌋
,→ nts[2]
142 values[str(n)]=float(env(1)‐modes@ref.radial_modes(np.array([p.R]))[:,0])
143 dump('modal_truncation_audit.json',dict(t_s=1,surface_C=values,
144 difference_320_vs_640=abs(values['320']‐values['640']),method='exact first‐interval PCHIP
,→ polynomial convolution, hypergeometric moments'))

1 export const requiredChecks = [
2 'event_found', 'finite', 'strict_below_all_verified_grids',
3 'strict_below_with_empirical_margin', 'moisture_bounds', 'temperature_bounds',

9 export function requireQ3Delivery(v) {
10 const gate = v?.delivery_gate;
11 if (v?.result_version !== 'q3‐closeout‐v1' || gate?.meets_delivery_gate !== true ||
12 !requiredChecks.every(name => gate?.checks?.[name] === true) ||
13 !Object.values(gate.checks).every(value => value === true)) {
14 throw new Error('Numerical validation failed. Q3 export blocked.');
15 }
16 }

补充对照程序与复现
附录 4 固定半径对照的完整程序如下。复算依次运行：
python code/q4_fixed_comparison.py ‐‐level 8
python code/q4_fixed_comparison.py ‐‐level 16
python code/q4_fixed_comparison.py ‐‐level 8 ‐‐tight
python code/q4_fixed_comparison.py ‐‐summarize
python code/plot_q4_fixed_comparison.py
汇总需先按前述步骤生成第四问原收缩结果。若仅复核图形，可直接使用支撑材料所附
的对照 JSON 与两组中心曲线 CSV；较大的固定组内部数组可重算。
1 """Appendix‐4 fixed‐radius control. Existing production results are read‐only.
2
3 Run ‐‐level 8, ‐‐level 16, ‐‐level 8 ‐‐tight, then ‐‐summarize.
4 The fixed control requires no extrapolation of the measured shrinking radius.
5 """
6 from pathlib import Path
7 import argparse
8 import csv
9 import hashlib
10 import json
11 import math
12 import time
13 import numpy as np
14 from numpy.polynomial.legendre import leggauss
15 from scipy.integrate import solve_ivp
16 from problem4 import Inputs, ShrinkingFVM, properties, SWITCH, THRESHOLD
17 from radial_fvm import graded_radial_nodes, harmonic_mean
97
18 from utils import RESULTS_DIR, R, T0, C0, H, HM

20 OUT = RESULTS_DIR / 'q4_fixed_comparison'

23 def dump(path, obj):
24 path.write_text(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf‐8')
25

27 def run(level, tight):
28 OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
29 inputs = Inputs()
30 fvm = ShrinkingFVM(graded_radial_nodes(level)/R)
31 n = fvm.n
32 label = f'G{level}' + ('_tight' if tight else '')
33 rtol = 1e‐9 if tight else 1e‐8
34 atol = np.r_[np.full(n, 1e‐9 if tight else 1e‐8), np.full(n, 1e‐11 if tight else 1e‐10)]
35 y = np.r_[np.full(n, T0), np.full(n, C0)]
36 times, states = [np.array([0.])], [y[None, :]]
37 event = lambda t, z: float(z[n:].max()‐THRESHOLD)
38 event.direction, event.terminal = ‐1., False
39 breaks = np.unique(np.r_[0., SWITCH, np.arange(21600., 864000.+1., 21600.)])
40 gx, gw = leggauss(4)
41 loss, mass_error, heat_error = 0., 0., 0.
42 bounds = [T0, T0, C0, C0]
43 segments = []
44 critical = None
45 started = time.perf_counter()
46 for a, b in zip(breaks[:‐1], breaks[1:]):
47 late = a >= SWITCH
48 def rhs(t, z):
49 ta, ca = inputs.environment(t, late=late)
50 return fvm.rhs(z, R, ta, ca)
51 step = (5. if tight else 10.) if not late else (30. if tight else 60.)
52 sol = solve_ivp(rhs, (a, b), y, method='BDF', rtol=rtol, atol=atol,
53 max_step=step, jac_sparsity=fvm.sparsity(), dense_output=True, events=event)
54 if not sol.success:
55 raise RuntimeError(sol.message)
56 ts = np.arange(a+60., b+.1, 60.)
57 yy = sol.sol(ts).T
58 times.append(ts); states.append(yy)
59 for start in range(0, len(sol.t)‐1, 128):
60 aa, bb = sol.t[start:‐1][:128], sol.t[start+1:][:128]
61 qq = ((aa+bb)[:,None]/2 + (bb‐aa)[:,None]/2*gx).ravel()
62 surf = sol.sol(qq)[‐1]
63 ca = np.full_like(qq, .05) if late else inputs.Ca(qq)
64 flux = 2*HM/R*(surf‐ca)
65 loss += float(np.sum(flux.reshape(‐1,4)*gw*(bb‐aa)[:,None]/2))
66 y = sol.y[:,‐1]
67 mass_error = max(mass_error, abs(float(2*np.dot(fvm.w,y[n:])‐C0+loss)))
68 bounds = [min(bounds[0],float(sol.y[:n].min())), max(bounds[1],float(sol.y[:n].max())),
69 min(bounds[2],float(sol.y[n:].min())), max(bounds[3],float(sol.y[n:].max()))]
70 for tq in (a,(a+b)/2,b):
71 z = sol.sol(tq); dz = rhs(tq,z); B,_,_ = properties(z[n:],z[:n])
72 ta,_ = inputs.environment(tq,late=late)
73 heat_error = max(heat_error,abs(float(np.dot(fvm.w*B,dz[:n])+H/R*(z[n‐1]‐ta))))
74 segments.append({'start_s':float(a),'end_s':float(b),'steps':len(sol.t)‐1,'mass_abs':mass_error})
75 print(f'{label}: {b/3600:.0f} h, maxC={y[n:].max():.8f}, wall={time.perf_counter()‐started:.1f}s',
,→ flush=True)
76 if len(sol.t_events[0]):
77 critical = float(sol.t_events[0][0])
78 event_state = sol.y_events[0][0]
79 break
80 if critical is None:
81 raise RuntimeError('Fixed control has not crossed threshold by the 240 h numerical cap')
98
82 tt, YY = np.concatenate(times), np.vstack(states)
83 if not np.all(np.isfinite(YY)):
84 raise RuntimeError('nonfinite state')
85 # Independent physical‐ring assembly verifies fixed‐radius transformation.
86 rp = R*fvm.xi; rf = (rp[:‐1]+rp[1:])/2
87 vol = np.diff(np.r_[0.,rf,R]**2)/2
88 z = np.r_[35.+10*fvm.xi**2, 1.7‐1.2*fvm.xi**2]
89 B,k,D = properties(z[n:],z[:n])
90 ft = np.r_[0.,‐rf*harmonic_mean(k[:‐1],k[1:])*np.diff(z[:n])/np.diff(rp),R*H*(z[n‐1]‐50.)]
91 fc = np.r_[0.,‐rf*harmonic_mean(D[:‐1],D[1:])*np.diff(z[n:])/np.diff(rp),R*HM*(z[‐1]‐.05)]
92 ref = np.r_[‐np.diff(ft)/(vol*B),‐np.diff(fc)/vol]
93 operator_error = float(np.max(abs(ref‐fvm.rhs(z,R,50.,.05))))
94 meta = {'label':label,'nodes':n,'rtol':rtol,'atol_T':float(atol[0]),'atol_C':float(atol[n]),
95 'critical_s':critical,'critical_h':critical/3600,'radius_m':R,'properties':'Appendix 4',
96 'input_audit':inputs.audit,'mass_relative_error':mass_error/C0,'heat_equation_residual':heat_e ⌋
,→ rror,
97 'physical_operator_error':operator_error,'bounds':bounds,'segments':segments,
98 'max_center_gap':float(np.max(YY[:,n:].max(axis=1)‐YY[:,n])),
99 'wall_s':time.perf_counter()‐started,
100 'source_sha256':{p.name:hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in
101 [Path(__file__),Path(__file__).with_name('problem4.py'),Path(__file__).with_n ⌋
,→ ame('radial_fvm.py')]}}
102 np.savez_compressed(OUT/f'{label}.npz',times_s=tt,Y=YY,xi=fvm.xi,event_state=event_state)
103 dump(OUT/f'{label}.json',meta)
104 print(json.dumps(meta,ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)

107 def state_at(data, t):
108 times = data['times_s']; i = int(np.searchsorted(times,t,side='right')‐1)
109 if i < 0 or t > times[‐1]:
110 raise ValueError('state query outside saved trajectory')
111 if abs(times[i]‐t)<1e‐8:
112 return data['Y'][i].copy()
113 assert times[i]>=SWITCH
114 fvm = ShrinkingFVM(data['xi']); n=fvm.n
115 sol = solve_ivp(lambda tt,y:fvm.rhs(y,R,50.,.05),(times[i],t),data['Y'][i],
116 method='BDF',rtol=1e‐10,atol=np.r_[np.full(n,1e‐10),np.full(n,1e‐12)],
117 max_step=1.,jac_sparsity=fvm.sparsity())
118 if not sol.success: raise RuntimeError(sol.message)
119 return sol.y[:,‐1]

122 def summarize():
123 labels = ['G8','G16','G8_tight']
124 ds = {k:np.load(OUT/f'{k}.npz') for k in labels}
125 ms = {k:json.loads((OUT/f'{k}.json').read_text(encoding='utf‐8')) for k in labels}
126 a,b,c = (ds[k] for k in labels); n,m = len(a['xi']),len(b['xi'])
127 assert np.allclose(a['xi'],b['xi'][::2],rtol=0,atol=1e‐14)
128 count = min(len(d['times_s']) for d in ds.values())
129 assert all(np.array_equal(a['times_s'][:count],d['times_s'][:count]) for d in ds.values())
130 space = [float(np.max(abs(a['Y'][:count,:n]‐b['Y'][:count,:m:2]))),

27 def make_case_functions(Ta0, Ca0, hm_scale):
28 Ta_base, Ca_base, _ = problem3.load_q3_inputs()
29
30 class Late:
31 def __init__(self, base, after):
32 self.base, self.after = base, float(after)
33
34 def __call__(self, t):
35 if np.ndim(t) == 0:
101
36 return float(self.base(t)) if float(t) <= problem3.T_SWITCH else self.after
37 t = np.asarray(t, dtype=float)
38 return np.where(t <= problem3.T_SWITCH, self.base(t), self.after)
39
40 return Late(Ta_base, Ta0), Late(Ca_base, Ca0), hm_scale

43 def run_case_sensitivity(level, Ta_fun, Ca_fun, hm_scale, tag):
44 """Use the production integrator with a scaled surface mass‐transfer coefficient."""
45 original = problem3.CoupledRadialFVM.rhs
46
47 def rhs_scaled(self, t, y, Ta, Ca, **kwargs):
48 # run_case supplies boundary functions; scale only the prescribed hm.
49 n = self.n_nodes
50 T, C = self.unpack(y)
51 kn = np.asarray(radial_coupled.conductivity(C), dtype=float)
52 Bn = np.asarray(radial_coupled.heat_capacity(C), dtype=float)
53 Dn = np.asarray(radial_coupled.moisture_diffusivity_q2(C, T, protect=True), dtype=float)
54 kf = radial_coupled.harmonic_mean(kn[:‐1], kn[1:])
55 Df = radial_coupled.harmonic_mean(Dn[:‐1], Dn[1:])
56 FT = ‐self.r_half*kf*np.diff(T)/self.dr_face
57 FC = ‐self.r_half*Df*np.diff(C)/self.dr_face
58 td = np.empty_like(T); cd = np.empty_like(C)
59 td[0] = ‐FT[0]/(Bn[0]*self.W[0]); cd[0] = ‐FC[0]/self.W[0]
60 td[1:‐1] = (FT[:‐1]‐FT[1:])/(Bn[1:‐1]*self.W[1:‐1])
61 cd[1:‐1] = (FC[:‐1]‐FC[1:])/self.W[1:‐1]
62 radius = float(self.r[‐1])
63 fts = radius*H*(T[‐1]‐Ta)
64 fcs = radius*(HM*hm_scale)*(C[‐1]‐Ca)
65 td[‐1] = (FT[‐1]‐fts)/(Bn[‐1]*self.W[‐1]); cd[‐1] = (FC[‐1]‐fcs)/self.W[‐1]
66 return self.pack(td, cd)
67
68 problem3.CoupledRadialFVM.rhs = rhs_scaled
69 try:
70 return problem3.run_case(level, Ta_fun, Ca_fun, 72*3600., problem3.RTOL,
71 problem3.ATOL_T, problem3.ATOL_C, problem3.MAX_STEP_EARLY,
72 problem3.MAX_STEP_LATE, tag=tag, audit_balances=False)
73 finally:
74 problem3.CoupledRadialFVM.rhs = original
75

77 def run_one(tag, ta, ca, scale):
78 taf, caf, hs = make_case_functions(ta, ca, scale)
79 result = run_case_sensitivity(8, taf, caf, hs, tag)
80 record = {
81 "case": tag, "Ta_after_C": ta, "Ca_after": ca, "hm_scale": scale,
82 "hm_window": "full_horizon", "Ca_window": "after_4h", "Ta_window": "after_4h",
83 "t_star_s": float(result["t_star"]), "t_star_h": float(result["t_star"]/3600),
84 "M_star": float(result["event"]["M_star"]), "rmax_star_cm": float(result["event"]["rmax_star_cm"]),
85 "nodes": int(result["n_nodes"]), "rtol": problem3.RTOL,
86 "atol_T": problem3.ATOL_T, "atol_C": problem3.ATOL_C,
87 }
88 (OUT / f"{tag}.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf‐8")
89 np.savez_compressed(OUT / f"{tag}.npz", times=result["times"], Y=result["Y"], r=result["fvm"].r,
90 t_star=result["t_star"], y_star=result["y_star"])
91 return record

,→ tive_change_pct']:.3f}" for r in rows]

31 def nodes(intervals: int, extent: float, power: float) ‐> np.ndarray:
32 if intervals < 4:
33 raise ValueError("At least four intervals are required")
34 u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, intervals + 1)
35 x = extent * (1.0 ‐ (1.0 ‐ u) ** power)
36 x[0], x[‐1] = 0.0, extent
37 return x

40 class AxisymmetricFVM:
41 def __init__(self, r, z, end_scale=1.0):
42 self.r, self.z = np.asarray(r, float), np.asarray(z, float)
43 if (len(self.r) < 3 or len(self.z) < 3 or self.r[0] != 0 or self.z[0] != 0
44 or self.r[‐1] != R or self.z[‐1] != Z
45 or np.any(np.diff(self.r) <= 0) or np.any(np.diff(self.z) <= 0)
46 or end_scale < 0):
47 raise ValueError("Invalid half‐cylinder grid or end coefficient")
48 self.nr, self.nz = len(self.r), len(self.z)
49 self.n = self.nr * self.nz
50 self.rf = (self.r[:‐1] + self.r[1:]) / 2.0
51 self.zf = (self.z[:‐1] + self.z[1:]) / 2.0
52 self.wr = np.diff(np.r_[0., self.rf, R] ** 2) / 2.0
53 self.wz = np.diff(np.r_[0., self.zf, Z])
54 self.volume = self.wr[:, None] * self.wz[None, :]
55 self.normalizer = R * R * Z / 2.0
56 self.end_scale = float(end_scale)
57 self.dr, self.dz = np.diff(self.r), np.diff(self.z)
58
59 def pack(self, T, C):
60 return np.stack((T, C), axis=‐1).ravel()
61
62 def unpack(self, y):
63 a = np.asarray(y).reshape(self.nr, self.nz, 2)
64 return a[:, :, 0], a[:, :, 1]
65
66 def divergence(self, state, coefficient, ambient, exchange):
67 fr = np.empty((self.nr + 1, self.nz))
68 fz = np.empty((self.nr, self.nz + 1))
69 fr[0] = 0.0
70 fr[1:‐1] = (‐self.rf[:, None]
71 * harmonic_mean(coefficient[:‐1], coefficient[1:])
72 * np.diff(state, axis=0) / self.dr[:, None])
73 fr[‐1] = R * exchange * (state[‐1] ‐ ambient)
74 fz[:, 0] = 0.0
75 fz[:, 1:‐1] = (‐harmonic_mean(coefficient[:, :‐1], coefficient[:, 1:])
76 * np.diff(state, axis=1) / self.dz[None, :])
77 fz[:, ‐1] = self.end_scale * exchange * (state[:, ‐1] ‐ ambient)
78 return ‐np.diff(fr, axis=0) / self.wr[:, None] ‐ np.diff(fz, axis=1) / self.wz[None, :]
79
80 def rhs(self, t, y, Ta, Ca):
81 T, C = self.unpack(y)
82 B = heat_capacity(C)
83 k = conductivity(C)
84 D = moisture_diffusivity_q2(C, T, protect=True)
85 return self.pack(self.divergence(T, k, Ta, H) / B,
86 self.divergence(C, D, Ca, HM))
87
88 def loss_rates(self, y, Ca):
89 # Rates normalized by dry mass; the unknown uniform dry density cancels.
90 C = self.unpack(y)[1]
91 side = R * HM * np.dot(self.wz, C[‐1] ‐ Ca) / self.normalizer
92 end = self.end_scale * HM * np.dot(self.wr, C[:, ‐1] ‐ Ca) / self.normalizer
93 return np.array([side, end])
104
94
95 def mean_C(self, y):
96 return float(np.sum(self.volume * self.unpack(y)[1]) / self.normalizer)
97
98 def sparsity(self):
99 ids = np.arange(self.n).reshape(self.nr, self.nz)
100 pairs = [(ids.ravel(), ids.ravel())]
101 for a, b in ((ids[:‐1].ravel(), ids[1:].ravel()),
102 (ids[:, :‐1].ravel(), ids[:, 1:].ravel())):
103 pairs.extend(((a, b), (b, a)))
104 rows, cols = [], []
105 for a, b in pairs:
106 for i in (0, 1):
107 for j in (0, 1):
108 rows.append(2 * a + i)
109 cols.append(2 * b + j)
110 row, col = np.concatenate(rows), np.concatenate(cols)
111 return coo_matrix((np.ones(len(row)), (row, col)), shape=(2*self.n, 2*self.n)).tocsc()
112
113
114 def structural_checks():
115 f = AxisymmetricFVM(nodes(12, R, 2), nodes(10, Z, 2.5))
116 rr, zz = f.r[:, None] / R, f.z[None, :] / Z
117 T = 32 + 8*rr**2 + 3*zz**2
118 C = 1.8 ‐ .4*rr**2 ‐ .3*zz**2
119 y = f.pack(T, C)
120 dy = f.rhs(0, y, 50., .05)
121 td, cd = f.unpack(dy)
122 mass = abs(np.sum(f.volume*cd)/f.normalizer + f.loss_rates(y,.05).sum())
123 heat_in = (R*H*np.dot(f.wz, 50‐T[‐1])
124 + H*np.dot(f.wr, 50‐T[:, ‐1]))
125 heat = abs(np.sum(f.volume*heat_capacity(C)*td) ‐ heat_in)
126
127 f0 = AxisymmetricFVM(f.r, f.z, 0.0)
128 radial = CoupledRadialFVM(f.r)
129 t1, c1 = 30 + 7*(f.r/R)**2, 2 ‐ .5*(f.r/R)**2
130 uniform_z = f0.pack(np.broadcast_to(t1[:, None], (f.nr,f.nz)),
131 np.broadcast_to(c1[:, None], (f.nr,f.nz)))
132 two = f0.unpack(f0.rhs(0,uniform_z,50.,.05))
133 one = radial.unpack(radial.rhs(0,radial.pack(t1,c1),50.,.05))
134 reduction = max(float(np.max(abs(a‐b[:,None]))) for a,b in zip(two,one))
135
136 # Constant‐coefficient polynomial has a known cylindrical Laplacian (4+2).
137 q = rr**2 + zz**2
138 lap = f.divergence(q, np.ones_like(q), 0., 0.)
139 exact = 4/R**2 + 2/Z**2
140 # On a uniform grid the interior polynomial is exact; graded node averages are approximate.
141 fu = AxisymmetricFVM(np.linspace(0,R,13), np.linspace(0,Z,11))
142 q = (fu.r[:,None]/R)**2 + (fu.z[None,:]/Z)**2
143 lap = fu.divergence(q, np.ones_like(q),0.,0.)
144 polynomial = float(np.max(abs(lap[:‐1,:‐1]‐exact))/exact)
145
146 # Check all structurally nonzero derivative locations against a numerical Jacobian.
147 pat = f.sparsity().toarray() != 0
148 missing = 0.0
149 eps = 1e‐6
150 for j in range(len(y)):
151 shifted = y.copy()
152 shifted[j] += eps
153 diff = (f.rhs(0,shifted,50.,.05)‐dy)/eps
154 missing = max(missing, float(np.max(abs(diff[~pat[:,j]]), initial=0.0)))
155 out = {"mass_rate_abs":float(mass), "effective_heat_rate_abs":float(heat),
156 "sealed_rhs_vs_independent_radial_max_abs":float(reduction),
157 "uniform_polynomial_relative_error":polynomial,
158 "jacobian_missing_entry_max_abs":missing,
105
159 "volume_relative_error":float(abs(f.volume.sum()/f.normalizer‐1))}
160 out["pass"] = bool(mass < 1e‐12 and heat < 1e‐10 and reduction < 1e‐10
161 and polynomial < 1e‐10 and missing < 1e‐10)
162 return out
163
164
165 def provenance():
166 files = [Path(__file__), Path(__file__).with_name("appendix3.py"),
167 Path(__file__).with_name("problem3.py"), Path(__file__).with_name("utils.py"),
168 ATTACH_DIR / " 附件 1.xlsx", RESULTS_DIR / "q3_validation.json"]
169 return {str(p):hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in files}

172 def run(nr, nz, end_scale=1.0, tight=False, tag=None, cap=CAP):
173 OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
174 tag = tag or f"r{nr}_z{nz}_end{end_scale:g}" + ("_tight" if tight else "")
175 f = AxisymmetricFVM(nodes(nr,R,2.0), nodes(nz,Z,2.5), end_scale)
176 Ta_fun, Ca_fun, _ = load_q3_inputs()
177 rtol = 1e‐8 if tight else 1e‐7
178 atol = np.tile([1e‐8 if tight else 1e‐7, 1e‐10 if tight else 1e‐9],f.n)
179 y = f.pack(np.full((f.nr,f.nz),T0), np.full((f.nr,f.nz),C0))
180 sparsity = f.sparsity()
181 history = []
182 snapshots = {}
183 snap_times = [12*3600., 36*3600., 48*3600.]
184 loss4, loss8 = np.zeros(2), np.zeros(2)
185 quad4, weight4 = leggauss(4)
186 quad8, weight8 = leggauss(8)
187 max_mass_res = 0.
188 bounds = [T0,T0,C0,C0]
189 max_radial_increase = max_axial_increase = max_center_gap = 0.
190 sealed_axial_spread = 0.
191 event_t, event_y = None, None
192 accepted, nfev, njev, nlu = 0,0,0,0
193 wall = time.perf_counter()
194 print_clock = wall
195 next_sample = 0.
196
197 def record(t, state):
198 nonlocal max_radial_increase, max_axial_increase, max_center_gap, sealed_axial_spread
199 T,C = f.unpack(state)
200 loc = np.unravel_index(np.argmax(C),C.shape)
201 max_radial_increase = max(max_radial_increase,float(np.max(np.diff(C,axis=0))))
202 max_axial_increase = max(max_axial_increase,float(np.max(np.diff(C,axis=1))))
203 max_center_gap = max(max_center_gap,float(C.max()‐C[0,0]))
204 if end_scale == 0:
205 sealed_axial_spread = max(sealed_axial_spread,float(np.ptp(C,axis=1).max()),
206 float(np.ptp(T,axis=1).max()))
207 history.append([t,C.max(),C[0,0],C[‐1,0],C[0,‐1],T[0,0],
208 f.mean_C(state),f.r[loc[0]],f.z[loc[1]]])
209
210 for start,end in ((0.,min(T_SWITCH,cap)),(T_SWITCH,cap)):
211 if start >= end or event_t is not None:
212 continue
213 late = start >= T_SWITCH
214 def environment(t):
215 return (TA_AFTER,CA_AFTER) if late else (float(Ta_fun(t)),float(Ca_fun(t)))
216 def rhs(t,state):
217 return f.rhs(t,state,*environment(t))
218 dt = (5. if tight else 10.) if not late else (30. if tight else 60.)
219 solver = BDF(rhs,start,y,end,rtol=rtol,atol=atol,max_step=dt,jac_sparsity=sparsity)
220 previous_M = float(f.unpack(y)[1].max())
221 while solver.status == "running":
222 a = solver.t
223 message = solver.step()
106
224 if solver.status == "failed":
225 raise RuntimeError(message)
226 accepted += 1
227 b = solver.t
228 dense = solver.dense_output()
229 T,C = f.unpack(solver.y)
230 if not np.all(np.isfinite(solver.y)) or C.min() <= 0:
231 raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite or nonpositive accepted state")
232 bounds = [min(bounds[0],float(T.min())),max(bounds[1],float(T.max())),
233 min(bounds[2],float(C.min())),max(bounds[3],float(C.max()))]
234 current_M = float(C.max())
235 if previous_M > THRESHOLD and current_M <= THRESHOLD:
236 event_t = brentq(lambda t:float(f.unpack(dense(t))[1].max()‐THRESHOLD),a,b,
237 xtol=1e‐6,rtol=1e‐13)
238 event_y = dense(event_t)
239 b = event_t
240 for gx,gw,total in ((quad4,weight4,loss4),(quad8,weight8,loss8)):
241 q = (a+b)/2+(b‐a)/2*gx
242 vals = dense(q)
243 rates = np.array([f.loss_rates(vals[:,j],environment(t)[1]) for j,t in enumerate(q)])
244 total += (b‐a)/2*np.sum(gw[:,None]*rates,axis=0)
245 state_b = event_y if event_t is not None else solver.y
246 residual = abs(f.mean_C(state_b)‐C0+loss8.sum())
247 max_mass_res = max(max_mass_res,residual)
248 while next_sample <= b+1e‐8:
249 if next_sample >= a‐1e‐8:
250 record(next_sample,dense(next_sample))
251 next_sample += 600.
252 for target in snap_times:
253 if a < target <= b:
254 snapshots[f"t{int(target)}"] = dense(target).reshape(f.nr,f.nz,2)
255 if time.perf_counter()‐print_clock > 25:
256 print(f"{tag}: t={b/3600:.3f} h maxC={f.unpack(state_b)[1].max():.8f} "
257 f"mass={residual:.2e} steps={accepted}
,→ wall={time.perf_counter()‐wall:.1f}s",flush=True)
258 print_clock = time.perf_counter()
259 if event_t is not None:
260 record(event_t,event_y)
261 snapshots["event"] = event_y.reshape(f.nr,f.nz,2)
262 break
263 previous_M = current_M
264 nfev += solver.nfev
265 njev += solver.njev
266 nlu += solver.nlu
267 y = solver.y.copy()
268 if event_t is None:
269 raise RuntimeError(f"Threshold not reached before {cap/3600:g} hours")
270 T,C = f.unpack(event_y)
271 loc = np.unravel_index(np.argmax(C),C.shape)
272 tolerance = 1e‐7
273 wet = np.argwhere(C.max()‐C <= tolerance)
274 meta = {"version":"q3‐axisymmetric‐v1","tag":tag,"nr":f.nr,"nz":f.nz,
275 "unknowns":2*f.n,"end_scale":end_scale,"tight":tight,
276 "r_mesh_power":2.0,"z_mesh_power":2.5,"rtol":rtol,
277 "atol_T":float(atol[0]),"atol_C":float(atol[1]),
278 "early_max_step_s":5. if tight else 10.,"late_max_step_s":30. if tight else 60.,
279 "t_star_s":float(event_t),"t_star_h":float(event_t/3600),
280 "event_max_C":float(C.max()),"event_center_C":float(C[0,0]),
281 "event_argmax_rz_m":[float(f.r[loc[0]]),float(f.z[loc[1]])],
282 "wet_region_tolerance_C":tolerance,
283 "event_wet_region_max_rz_m":[float(f.r[wet[:,0]].max()),float(f.z[wet[:,1]].max())],
284 "mean_C_event":f.mean_C(event_y),"side_loss_per_dry_mass":float(loss8[0]),
285 "ends_loss_per_dry_mass":float(loss8[1]),
286 "mass_balance_max_abs":max_mass_res,"mass_balance_relative_initial":max_mass_res/C0,
287 "mass_balance_relative_loss":max_mass_res/(C0‐f.mean_C(event_y)),
107
288 "quadrature_4_vs_8_abs":float(np.max(abs(loss4‐loss8))),
289 "bounds_Tmin_Tmax_Cmin_Cmax":bounds,
290 "sampled_max_radial_C_increase":max_radial_increase,
291 "sampled_max_axial_C_increase":max_axial_increase,
292 "sampled_max_C_minus_center":max_center_gap,
293 "sealed_sampled_axial_spread":sealed_axial_spread,
294 "accepted_steps":accepted,"nfev":nfev,"njev":njev,"nlu":nlu,
295 "wall_s":time.perf_counter()‐wall,"python":platform.python_version(),
296 "numpy":np.__version__,"scipy":scipy.__version__,"source_sha256":provenance(),
297 "history_columns":["time_s","max_C","center_C","side_mid_C","end_axis_C",
298 "center_T","mean_C","argmax_r_m","argmax_z_m"],
299 "limitations":["effective no‐latent baseline","equal end/side coefficients in open case",
300 "isotropic coefficients","identical end environments","Q3 only"]}
301 meta["numerical_checks_pass"] = bool(max_mass_res/C0 < 1e‐6 and abs(C.max()‐.15) < 1e‐9
302 and bounds[2] > 0 and bounds[3] < C0+1e‐7)
303 np.savez_compressed(OUT/f"{tag}.npz",r=f.r,z=f.z,wr=f.wr,wz=f.wz,
304 history=np.asarray(history),**snapshots)
305 (OUT/f"{tag}.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding="utf‐8")
306 print(json.dumps({k:meta[k] for k in ("tag","t_star_h","mass_balance_relative_loss",
307 "event_argmax_rz_m","wall_s","numerical_checks_pass")}),flush=Tru ⌋
,→ e)
308 return meta

311 def paired_radial(nr=80,tight=False):
312 f = CoupledRadialFVM(nodes(nr,R,2.0))
313 Ta,Ca,_ = load_q3_inputs()
314 y = f.pack(np.full(f.n_nodes,T0),np.full(f.n_nodes,C0))
315 histories = []
316 event_t = None
317 start_wall = time.perf_counter()
318 for a,b in ((0.,T_SWITCH),(T_SWITCH,CAP)):
319 late = a >= T_SWITCH
320 def rhs(t,state):
321 return f.rhs(t,state,TA_AFTER if late else float(Ta(t)),CA_AFTER if late else float(Ca(t)))
322 def event(t,state):
323 return float(f.unpack(state)[1].max()‐.15)
324 event.terminal,event.direction = True,‐1
325 sol = solve_ivp(rhs,(a,b),y,method="BDF",rtol=1e‐8 if tight else 1e‐7,
326 atol=np.r_[np.full(f.n_nodes,1e‐8 if tight else 1e‐7),
327 np.full(f.n_nodes,1e‐10 if tight else 1e‐9)],
328 max_step=(5. if tight else 10.) if not late else (30. if tight else 60.),
329 jac_sparsity=f.jac_sparsity(),dense_output=True,events=event)
330 if not sol.success:
331 raise RuntimeError(sol.message)
332 ts = np.arange(a,sol.t[‐1]+1e‐8,600.)
333 vals = sol.sol(ts)
334 histories.extend(np.column_stack([ts,vals[f.n_nodes:].max(axis=0),vals[0]]).tolist())
335 y = sol.y[:,‐1]
336 if len(sol.t_events[0]):
337 event_t = float(sol.t_events[0][0])
338 histories.append([event_t,float(y[f.n_nodes:].max()),float(y[0])])
339 break
340 if event_t is None:
341 raise RuntimeError("Radial reference did not reach the threshold")
342 label = f"radial_r{nr}"+("_tight" if tight else "")
343 meta = {"tag":label,"nr":f.n_nodes,"tight":tight,"t_star_s":event_t,"t_star_h":event_t/3600,
344 "wall_s":time.perf_counter()‐start_wall,"source_sha256":provenance()}
345 OUT.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
346 np.savez_compressed(OUT/f"{label}.npz",r=f.r,event=y,history=np.asarray(histories))
347 (OUT/f"{label}.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding="utf‐8")
348 print(json.dumps(meta),flush=True)
349 return meta

20 def summarize(production="r160_z80_end1", tight="r160_z80_end1_tight"):
21 original = json.loads((RESULTS_DIR/"q3_validation.json").read_text(encoding="utf‐8"))
22 structural = json.loads((OUT/"structural_checks.json").read_text(encoding="utf‐8"))
23 labels = ["r80_z40_end1","r160_z40_end1","r80_z80_end1",production,tight]
24 labels = list(dict.fromkeys(labels))
25 cases = {label:read_case(label) for label in labels}
26 sealed = read_case("r80_z40_end0")
27 radial80 = read_case("radial_r80")
28 rows = []
29 for label,d in cases.items():
30 radial = read_case(f"radial_r{d['nr']‐1}" + ("_tight" if d["tight"] else ""))
31 delta = d["t_star_s"]‐radial["t_star_s"]
32 rows.append({"label":label,"nr":d["nr"],"nz":d["nz"],"t_star_h":d["t_star_h"],
33 "paired_radial_h":radial["t_star_h"],"end_effect_s":delta,

35 "difference_from_official_s":d["t_star_s"]‐original["t_star_s"],

109

,→ hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==sha
51 for p,sha in d["source_sha256"].items())

55 other = np.load(OUT/f"{label}.npz")

61 "times_h":[12,36,48],"comparison":"shared nodes at identical physical times"}

,→ "]+base["side_loss_per_dry_mass"]),

12 def main():
13 summary=json.loads((OUT/"comparison.json").read_text(encoding="utf‐8"))
